===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3216-3257
Documentation:
/**
   * @returns false iff the result of the expression is not consumed.
   */
Method code:
3216 |   static boolean isExpressionResultUsed(Node expr) {
3217 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider sharing some code with trySimpleUnusedResult.
3218 |     Node parent = expr.getParent();
3219 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
3220 |       case Token.BLOCK:
3221 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
3222 |         return false;
3223 |       case Token.CAST:
3224 |         return isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3225 |       case Token.HOOK:
3226 |       case Token.AND:
3227 |       case Token.OR:
3228 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3229 |             ? true : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3230 |       case Token.COMMA:
3231 |         Node gramps = parent.getParent();
3232 |         if (gramps.isCall() &&
3233 |             parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
3234 |           // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
3235 |           // call to an eval. See ECMA-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's OK for the first
3236 |           // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
3237 |           // an eval. This we pretend that this is "used".
3238 |           if (expr == parent.getFirstChild() &&
3239 |               parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
3240 |               expr.getNext().isName() &&
3241 |               "eval".equals(expr.getNext().getString())) {
3242 |             return true;
3243 |           }
3244 |         }
3245 | 
3246 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3247 |             ? false : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3248 |       case Token.FOR:
3249 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
3250 |           // Only an expression whose result is in the condition part of the
3251 |           // expression is used.
3252 |           return (parent.getChildAtIndex(1) == expr);
3253 |         }
3254 |         break;
3255 |     }
3256 |     return true;
3257 |   }

Method code:
117 |     static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
118 |       final String defaultResource
119 |           = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
120 | 
121 |       Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
122 | 
123 |       // ResourceBundle does caching.
124 |       ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
125 | 
126 |       String formatString;
127 |       try {
128 |           formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
129 |       } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
130 |           throw new RuntimeException
131 |               ("no message resource found for message property " + messageId);
132 |       }
133 | 
134 |       /*
135 |        * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
136 |        * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
137 |        * single 's.
138 |        */
139 |       MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
140 |       return formatter.format(arguments);
141 |     }

/**
   * Query the flag for the output charset.
   *
   * Let the outputCharset be the same as the input charset... except if
   * we're reading in UTF-8 by default.  By tradition, we've always
   * output ASCII to avoid various hiccups with different browsers,
   * proxies and firewalls.
   *
   * @return Name of the charset to use when writing outputs. Guaranteed to
   *    be a supported charset.
   * @throws FlagUsageException if flag is not a valid Charset name.
   */
Method code:
982 |   private String getLegacyOutputCharset() throws FlagUsageException {
983 |     if (!config.charset.isEmpty()) {
984 |       if (!Charset.isSupported(config.charset)) {
985 |         throw new FlagUsageException(config.charset +
986 |             " is not a valid charset name.");
987 |       }
988 |       return config.charset;
989 |     }
990 |     return "US-ASCII";
991 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 525-592
Documentation:

Method code:
525 |     @Override
526 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
527 |       if (isCallToScopeMethod(n)) {
528 |         validateScopeCall(t, n, n.getParent());
529 |       }
530 | 
531 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() < 2) {
532 |         return;
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       int type = n.getType();
536 |       Var aliasVar = null;
537 |       if (type == Token.NAME) {
538 |         String name = n.getString();
539 |         Var lexicalVar = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
540 |         if (lexicalVar != null && lexicalVar == aliases.get(name)) {
541 |           aliasVar = lexicalVar;
542 |         }
543 |       }
544 | 
545 |       // Validate the top-level of the goog.scope block.
546 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() == 2) {
547 |         if (aliasVar != null && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
548 |           if (aliasVar.getNode() == n) {
549 |             aliasDefinitionsInOrder.add(n);
550 | 
551 |             // Return early, to ensure that we don't record a definition
552 |             // twice.
553 |             return;
554 |           } else {
555 |             report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_REDEFINED, n.getString());
556 |           }
557 |         }
558 | 
559 |         if (type == Token.RETURN) {
560 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_RETURN);
561 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
562 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_REFERENCES_THIS);
563 |         } else if (type == Token.THROW) {
564 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_THROW);
565 |         }
566 |       }
567 | 
568 |       // Validate all descendent scopes of the goog.scope block.
569 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() >= 2) {
570 |         // Check if this name points to an alias.
571 |         if (aliasVar != null) {
572 |           // Note, to support the transitive case, it's important we don't
573 |           // clone aliasedNode here.  For example,
574 |           // var g = goog; var d = g.dom; d.createElement('DIV');
575 |           // The node in aliasedNode (which is "g") will be replaced in the
576 |           // changes pass above with "goog".  If we cloned here, we'd end up
577 |           // with <code>g.dom.createElement('DIV')</code>.
578 |           aliasUsages.add(new AliasedNode(aliasVar, n));
579 |         }
580 | 
581 |         // When we inject declarations, we duplicate jsdoc. Make sure
582 |         // we only process that jsdoc once.
583 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
584 |         if (info != null && !injectedDecls.contains(n)) {
585 |           for (Node node : info.getTypeNodes()) {
586 |             fixTypeNode(node);
587 |           }
588 |         }
589 | 
590 |         // TODO(robbyw): Error for goog.scope not at root.
591 |       }
592 |     }

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 318-357
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
318 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
319 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
320 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
321 |       options.checkTypes = true;
322 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
323 |       options.checkTypes = false;
324 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
325 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
326 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
327 |       // parser type warnings.
328 |       options.setWarningLevel(
329 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
330 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
331 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
335 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
336 |       options.setWarningLevel(
337 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
338 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
339 |     }
340 | 
341 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
342 |       options.setWarningLevel(
343 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
344 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
348 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
349 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
350 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
351 |     // error off.
352 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
353 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
354 |       options.setWarningLevel(
355 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
356 |     }
357 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0050 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsMessageVisitor.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsMessageVisitor.java
Original range: 195-314
Documentation:

Method code:
195 |   @Override
196 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
197 |     String messageKey;
198 |     boolean isVar;
199 |     Node msgNode, msgNodeParent;
200 | 
201 |     switch (node.getType()) {
202 |       case Token.NAME:
203 |         // var MSG_HELLO = 'Message'
204 |         if ((parent != null) && (parent.isVar())) {
205 |           messageKey = node.getString();
206 |           isVar = true;
207 |         } else {
208 |           return;
209 |         }
210 | 
211 |         msgNode = node.getFirstChild();
212 |         msgNodeParent = node;
213 |         break;
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
215 |         // somenamespace.someclass.MSG_HELLO = 'Message'
216 |         isVar = false;
217 | 
218 |         Node getProp = node.getFirstChild();
219 |         if (!getProp.isGetProp()) {
220 |           return;
221 |         }
222 | 
223 |         Node propNode = getProp.getLastChild();
224 | 
225 |         messageKey = propNode.getString();
226 |         msgNode = node.getLastChild();
227 |         msgNodeParent = node;
228 |         break;
229 |       case Token.CALL:
230 |         // goog.getMsg()
231 |         String fnName = node.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
232 |         if (MSG_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
233 |           googMsgNodes.put(node, traversal.getSourceName());
234 |         } else if (MSG_FALLBACK_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
235 |           visitFallbackFunctionCall(traversal, node);
236 |         }
237 |         return;
238 |       default:
239 |         return;
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // Is this a message name?
243 |     boolean isNewStyleMessage =
244 |         msgNode != null && msgNode.isCall();
245 |     if (!isMessageName(messageKey, isNewStyleMessage)) {
246 |       return;
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (msgNode == null) {
250 |       compiler.report(
251 |           traversal.makeError(node, MESSAGE_HAS_NO_VALUE, messageKey));
252 |       return;
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     // Just report a warning if a qualified messageKey that looks like a message
256 |     // (e.g. "a.b.MSG_X") doesn't use goog.getMsg().
257 |     if (isNewStyleMessage) {
258 |       googMsgNodes.remove(msgNode);
259 |     } else if (style != JsMessage.Style.LEGACY) {
260 |       compiler.report(traversal.makeError(node, checkLevel,
261 |           MESSAGE_NOT_INITIALIZED_USING_NEW_SYNTAX));
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     boolean isUnnamedMsg = isUnnamedMessageName(messageKey);
265 | 
266 |     Builder builder = new Builder(
267 |         isUnnamedMsg ? null : messageKey);
268 |     builder.setSourceName(traversal.getSourceName());
269 | 
270 |     try {
271 |       if (isVar) {
272 |         extractMessageFromVariable(builder, node, parent, parent.getParent());
273 |       } else {
274 |         extractMessageFromProperty(builder, node.getFirstChild(), node);
275 |       }
276 |     } catch (MalformedException ex) {
277 |       compiler.report(traversal.makeError(ex.getNode(),
278 |           MESSAGE_TREE_MALFORMED, ex.getMessage()));
279 |       return;
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     JsMessage extractedMessage = builder.build(idGenerator);
283 | 
284 |     // If asked to check named internal messages.
285 |     if (needToCheckDuplications
286 |         && !isUnnamedMsg
287 |         && !extractedMessage.isExternal()) {
288 |       checkIfMessageDuplicated(messageKey, msgNode);
289 |     }
290 |     trackMessage(traversal, extractedMessage,
291 |         messageKey, msgNode, isUnnamedMsg);
292 | 
293 |     if (extractedMessage.isEmpty()) {
294 |       // value of the message is an empty string. Translators do not like it.
295 |       compiler.report(traversal.makeError(node, MESSAGE_HAS_NO_TEXT,
296 |           messageKey));
297 |     }
298 | 
299 |     // New-style messages must have descriptions. We don't emit a warning
300 |     // for legacy-style messages, because there are thousands of
301 |     // them in legacy code that are not worth the effort to fix, since they've
302 |     // already been translated anyway.
303 |     String desc = extractedMessage.getDesc();
304 |     if (isNewStyleMessage
305 |         && (desc == null || desc.trim().isEmpty())
306 |         && !extractedMessage.isExternal()) {
307 |       compiler.report(traversal.makeError(node, MESSAGE_HAS_NO_DESCRIPTION,
308 |           messageKey));
309 |     }
310 | 
311 |     JsMessageDefinition msgDefinition = new JsMessageDefinition(
312 |         node, msgNode, msgNodeParent);
313 |     processJsMessage(extractedMessage, msgDefinition);
314 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.deps.SortedDependencies.SortedDependencies(List<INPUT>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/deps/SortedDependencies.java
Original range: 64-108
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   public SortedDependencies(List<INPUT> inputs)
65 |       throws CircularDependencyException {
66 |     this.inputs = Lists.newArrayList(inputs);
67 |     noProvides = Lists.newArrayList();
68 | 
69 |     // Collect all symbols provided in these files.
70 |     for (INPUT input : inputs) {
71 |       Collection<String> currentProvides = input.getProvides();
72 |       if (currentProvides.isEmpty()) {
73 |         noProvides.add(input);
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       for (String provide : currentProvides) {
77 |         provideMap.put(provide, input);
78 |       }
79 |     }
80 | 
81 |     // Get the direct dependencies.
82 |     final Multimap<INPUT, INPUT> deps = HashMultimap.create();
83 |     for (INPUT input : inputs) {
84 |       for (String req : input.getRequires()) {
85 |         INPUT dep = provideMap.get(req);
86 |         if (dep != null && dep != input) {
87 |           deps.put(input, dep);
88 |         }
89 |       }
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     // Sort the inputs by sucking in 0-in-degree nodes until we're done.
93 |     sortedList = topologicalStableSort(inputs, deps);
94 | 
95 |     // The dependency graph of inputs has a cycle iff sortedList is a proper
96 |     // subset of inputs. Also, it has a cycle iff the subgraph
97 |     // (inputs - sortedList) has a cycle. It's fairly easy to prove this
98 |     // by the lemma that a graph has a cycle iff it has a subgraph where
99 |     // no nodes have out-degree 0. I'll leave the proof of this as an exercise
100 |     // to the reader.
101 |     if (sortedList.size() < inputs.size()) {
102 |       List<INPUT> subGraph = Lists.newArrayList(inputs);
103 |       subGraph.removeAll(sortedList);
104 | 
105 |       throw new CircularDependencyException(
106 |           cycleToString(findCycle(subGraph, deps)));
107 |     }
108 |   }

===== M0081 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsMessageVisitor.extractMessageFromVariable(Builder,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsMessageVisitor.java
Original range: 383-409
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a {@link JsMessage} for a JS message defined using a JS variable
   * declaration (e.g <code>var MSG_X = ...;</code>).
   *
   * @param builder the message builder
   * @param nameNode a NAME node for a JS message variable
   * @param parentNode a VAR node, parent of {@code nameNode}
   * @param grandParentNode the grandparent of {@code nameNode}. This node is
   *        only used to get meta data about the message that might be
   *        surrounding it (e.g. a message description). This argument may be
   *        null if the meta data is not needed.
   * @throws MalformedException if {@code varNode} does not
   *         correspond to a valid JS message VAR node
   */
Method code:
383 |   private void extractMessageFromVariable(
384 |       Builder builder, Node nameNode, Node parentNode,
385 |       @Nullable Node grandParentNode) throws MalformedException {
386 | 
387 |     // Determine the message's value
388 |     Node valueNode = nameNode.getFirstChild();
389 |     switch (valueNode.getType()) {
390 |       case Token.STRING:
391 |       case Token.ADD:
392 |         maybeInitMetaDataFromJsDocOrHelpVar(builder, parentNode,
393 |             grandParentNode);
394 |         builder.appendStringPart(extractStringFromStringExprNode(valueNode));
395 |         break;
396 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
397 |         maybeInitMetaDataFromJsDocOrHelpVar(builder, parentNode,
398 |             grandParentNode);
399 |         extractFromFunctionNode(builder, valueNode);
400 |         break;
401 |       case Token.CALL:
402 |         maybeInitMetaDataFromJsDoc(builder, parentNode);
403 |         extractFromCallNode(builder, valueNode);
404 |         break;
405 |       default:
406 |         throw new MalformedException("Cannot parse value of message "
407 |             + builder.getKey(), valueNode);
408 |     }
409 |   }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 391-435
Documentation:

Method code:
391 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
392 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
393 |       cursor++;
394 |       --ungetCursor;
395 |       if (charno == -1) {
396 |         charno = getOffset();
397 |       }
398 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
399 |     }
400 | 
401 |     for (;;) {
402 |       int c;
403 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
404 |         hitEOF = true;
405 |         if (charno == -1) {
406 |           charno = getOffset();
407 |         }
408 |         return EOF_CHAR;
409 |       }
410 |       cursor++;
411 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
412 | 
413 | 
414 |       if (c <= 127) {
415 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
416 |           lineEndChar = c;
417 |           c = '\n';
418 |         }
419 |       } else {
420 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
421 |           continue;
422 |         }
423 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
424 |           lineEndChar = c;
425 |           c = '\n';
426 |         }
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       if (charno == -1) {
430 |         charno = getOffset();
431 |       }
432 | 
433 |       return c;
434 |     }
435 |   }

===== M0129 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 81-110
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     int startErrorCount = compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount();
83 |     try {
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null ||
97 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
98 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
99 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
100 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
101 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
102 |       root = IR.script();
103 |     } else {
104 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
108 |     // the source file and module.
109 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
110 |   }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.deps.SortedDependencies.getDependenciesOf(List<INPUT>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/deps/SortedDependencies.java
Original range: 210-233
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the dependencies of the given roots. The inputs must be returned
   * in a stable order. In other words, if A comes before B, and A does not
   * transitively depend on B, then A must also come before B in the returned
   * list.
   *
   * @param sorted If true, get them in topologically sorted order. If false,
   *     get them in the original order they were passed to the compiler.
   */
Method code:
210 |   public List<INPUT> getDependenciesOf(List<INPUT> roots, boolean sorted) {
211 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(inputs.containsAll(roots));
212 |     Set<INPUT> included = Sets.newHashSet();
213 |     Deque<INPUT> worklist = new ArrayDeque<INPUT>(roots);
214 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
215 |       INPUT current = worklist.pop();
216 |       if (included.add(current)) {
217 |         for (String req : current.getRequires()) {
218 |           INPUT dep = provideMap.get(req);
219 |           if (dep != null) {
220 |             worklist.add(dep);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |     }
225 | 
226 |     ImmutableList.Builder<INPUT> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
227 |     for (INPUT current : (sorted ? sortedList : inputs)) {
228 |       if (included.contains(current)) {
229 |         builder.add(current);
230 |       }
231 |     }
232 |     return builder.build();
233 |   }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 307-366
Documentation:

Method code:
307 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
308 |     if (info.hasType()) {
309 |       boolean valid = false;
310 |       switch (node.getType()) {
311 |         // Casts are valid
312 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
313 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
314 |           break;
315 |         // Variable declarations are valid
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
317 |           valid = true;
318 |           break;
319 |         // Function declarations are valid
320 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
321 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode) node;
322 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
323 |           break;
324 |         // Object literal properties, catch declarations and variable
325 |         // initializers are valid.
326 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
327 |           AstNode parent = node.getParent();
328 |           valid = parent instanceof ObjectProperty
329 |               || parent instanceof CatchClause
330 |               || parent instanceof FunctionNode
331 |               || (parent instanceof VariableInitializer &&
332 |                   node == ((VariableInitializer) parent).getTarget());
333 |           break;
334 |         // Object literal properties are valid
335 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
336 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
337 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
338 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
339 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
340 |           break;
341 | 
342 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
343 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
344 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
345 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
346 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment) node).getLeft());
347 |           }
348 |           break;
349 | 
350 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
351 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
352 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
353 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
354 |           break;
355 | 
356 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CALL:
357 |           valid = info.isDefine();
358 |           break;
359 |       }
360 |       if (!valid) {
361 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
362 |             sourceName,
363 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
364 |       }
365 |     }
366 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerInput.regenerateDependencyInfoIfNecessary()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerInput.java
Original range: 206-251
Documentation:
/**
   * Regenerates the provides/requires if we need to do so.
   */
Method code:
206 |   private void regenerateDependencyInfoIfNecessary() throws IOException {
207 |     // If the code is NOT a JsAst, then it was not originally JS code.
208 |     // Look at the Ast for dependency info.
209 |     if (!(ast instanceof JsAst)) {
210 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(compiler,
211 |           "Expected setCompiler to be called first");
212 |       DepsFinder finder = new DepsFinder();
213 |       Node root = getAstRoot(compiler);
214 |       if (root == null) {
215 |         return;
216 |       }
217 | 
218 |       finder.visitTree(getAstRoot(compiler));
219 | 
220 |       // TODO(nicksantos|user): This caching behavior is a bit
221 |       // odd, and only works if you assume the exact call flow that
222 |       // clients are currently using.  In that flow, they call
223 |       // getProvides(), then remove the goog.provide calls from the
224 |       // AST, and then call getProvides() again.
225 |       //
226 |       // This won't work for any other call flow, or any sort of incremental
227 |       // compilation scheme. The API needs to be fixed so callers aren't
228 |       // doing weird things like this, and then we should get rid of the
229 |       // multiple-scan strategy.
230 | 
231 |       provides.addAll(finder.provides);
232 |       requires.addAll(finder.requires);
233 |     } else {
234 |       // Otherwise, look at the source code.
235 |       if (!generatedDependencyInfoFromSource) {
236 |         // Note: it's OK to use getName() instead of
237 |         // getPathRelativeToClosureBase() here because we're not using
238 |         // this to generate deps files. (We're only using it for
239 |         // symbol dependencies.)
240 |         DependencyInfo info =
241 |             (new JsFileParser(compiler.getErrorManager()))
242 |             .setIncludeGoogBase(true)
243 |             .parseFile(getName(), getName(), getCode());
244 | 
245 |         provides.addAll(info.getProvides());
246 |         requires.addAll(info.getRequires());
247 | 
248 |         generatedDependencyInfoFromSource = true;
249 |       }
250 |     }
251 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.protectSideEffects()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 137-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Protect side-effect free nodes by making them parameters
   * to a extern function call.  This call will be removed
   * after all the optimizations passes have run.
   */
Method code:
137 |   private void protectSideEffects() {
138 |     if (!problemNodes.isEmpty()) {
139 |       addExtern();
140 |       for (Node n : problemNodes) {
141 |         Node name = IR.name(PROTECTOR_FN).srcref(n);
142 |         name.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
143 |         Node replacement = IR.call(name).srcref(n);
144 |         replacement.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
145 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
146 |         replacement.addChildToBack(n);
147 |       }
148 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
149 |     }
150 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *

   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.

Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 |

121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 |

125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 967-1030
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
967 |     void replace() {
968 |       if (firstNode == null) {
969 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
970 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
971 |         return;
972 |       }
973 | 
974 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
975 |       // provided symbol.
976 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
977 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
978 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
979 | 
980 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
981 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
982 |         if (candidateDefinition.isExprResult() &&
983 |             !candidateDefinition.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
984 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
985 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
986 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
987 |           if (nameNode.isName()) {
988 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
989 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
990 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
991 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
992 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
993 |             Node varNode = IR.var(nameNode);
994 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
995 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
996 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
997 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
998 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
999 |             replacementNode = varNode;
1000 |           }
1001 |         }
1002 |       } else {
1003 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
1004 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
1005 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
1006 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
1007 |         } else {
1008 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
1009 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
1010 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.lastIndexOf('.');
1011 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
1012 |             // Any old place is fine.
1013 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
1014 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
1015 |           } else {
1016 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
1017 |             ProvidedName parentName =
1018 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
1019 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
1020 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
1021 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
1022 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
1023 |           }
1024 |         }
1025 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
1026 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
1027 |         }
1028 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
1029 |       }
1030 |     }

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.manageDependencies(DependencyOptions,List<CompilerInput>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 367-432
Documentation:
/**
   * Apply the dependency options to the list of sources, returning a new
   * source list re-ordering and dropping files as necessary.
   * This module graph will be updated to reflect the new list.
   *
   * @param inputs The original list of sources. Used to ensure that the sort
   *     is stable.
   * @throws CircularDependencyException if there is a circular dependency
   *     between the provides and requires.
   * @throws MissingProvideException if an entry point was not provided
   *     by any of the inputs.
   * @see DependencyOptions for more info on how this works.
   */
Method code:
367 |   public List<CompilerInput> manageDependencies(
368 |       DependencyOptions depOptions,
369 |       List<CompilerInput> inputs)
370 |       throws CircularDependencyException, MissingProvideException,
371 |           MissingModuleException {
372 | 
373 |     SortedDependencies<CompilerInput> sorter =
374 |         new SortedDependencies<CompilerInput>(inputs);
375 |     Iterable<CompilerInput> entryPointInputs = createEntryPointInputs(
376 |         depOptions, inputs, sorter);
377 | 
378 |     // The order of inputs, sorted independently of modules.
379 |     List<CompilerInput> absoluteOrder =
380 |         sorter.getDependenciesOf(inputs, depOptions.shouldSortDependencies());
381 | 
382 |     // Figure out which sources *must* be in each module.
383 |     ListMultimap<JSModule, CompilerInput> entryPointInputsPerModule =
384 |         LinkedListMultimap.create();
385 |     for (CompilerInput input : entryPointInputs) {
386 |       JSModule module = input.getModule();
387 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(module);
388 |       entryPointInputsPerModule.put(module, input);
389 |     }
390 | 
391 |     // Clear the modules of their inputs. This also nulls out
392 |     // the input's reference to its module.
393 |     for (JSModule module : getAllModules()) {
394 |       module.removeAll();
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     // Figure out which sources *must* be in each module, or in one
398 |     // of that module's dependencies.
399 |     for (JSModule module : entryPointInputsPerModule.keySet()) {
400 |       List<CompilerInput> transitiveClosure =
401 |           sorter.getDependenciesOf(
402 |               entryPointInputsPerModule.get(module),
403 |               depOptions.shouldSortDependencies());
404 |       for (CompilerInput input : transitiveClosure) {
405 |         JSModule oldModule = input.getModule();
406 |         if (oldModule == null) {
407 |           input.setModule(module);
408 |         } else {
409 |           input.setModule(null);
410 |           input.setModule(
411 |               getDeepestCommonDependencyInclusive(oldModule, module));
412 |         }
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     // All the inputs are pointing to the modules that own them. Yeah!
417 |     // Update the modules to reflect this.
418 |     for (CompilerInput input : absoluteOrder) {
419 |       JSModule module = input.getModule();
420 |       if (module != null) {
421 |         module.add(input);
422 |       }
423 |     }
424 | 
425 |     // Now, generate the sorted result.
426 |     List<CompilerInput> result = Lists.newArrayList();
427 |     for (JSModule module : getAllModules()) {
428 |       result.addAll(module.getInputs());
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     return result;
432 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runInCompilerThread(Callable<T>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 677-744
Documentation:

Method code:
677 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
678 |   <T> T runInCompilerThread(final Callable<T> callable) {
679 |     T result = null;
680 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
681 | 
682 |     Preconditions.checkState(
683 |         compilerThread == null || compilerThread == Thread.currentThread(),
684 |         "Please do not share the Compiler across threads");
685 | 
686 |     // If the compiler thread is available, use it.
687 |     if (useThreads && compilerThread == null) {
688 |       try {
689 |         final boolean dumpTraceReport =
690 |             options != null && options.tracer.isOn();
691 |         Callable<T> bootCompilerThread = new Callable<T>() {
692 |           @Override
693 |           public T call() {
694 |             try {
695 |               compilerThread = Thread.currentThread();
696 |               if (dumpTraceReport) {
697 |                 Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
698 |               }
699 |               return callable.call();
700 |             } catch (Throwable e) {
701 |               exception[0] = e;
702 |             } finally {
703 |               compilerThread = null;
704 |               if (dumpTraceReport) {
705 |                 Tracer.logCurrentThreadTrace();
706 |                 tracker.outputTracerReport(outStream == null ?
707 |                     System.out : outStream);
708 |               }
709 |               Tracer.clearCurrentThreadTrace();
710 |             }
711 |             return null;
712 |           }
713 |         };
714 | 
715 |         Future<T> future = compilerExecutor.submit(bootCompilerThread);
716 |         if (timeout > 0) {
717 |           result = future.get(timeout, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
718 |         } else {
719 |           result = future.get();
720 |         }
721 |       } catch (InterruptedException e) {
722 |         throw Throwables.propagate(e);
723 |       } catch (ExecutionException e) {
724 |         throw Throwables.propagate(e);
725 |       } catch (TimeoutException e) {
726 |         throw Throwables.propagate(e);
727 |       }
728 |     } else {
729 |       try {
730 |         result = callable.call();
731 |       } catch (Exception e) {
732 |         exception[0] = e;
733 |       } finally {
734 |         Tracer.clearCurrentThreadTrace();
735 |       }
736 |     }
737 | 
738 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
739 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
740 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
741 |     }
742 | 
743 |     return result;
744 |   }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-101
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       boolean visitedFunction = false;
56 |       for (Node current = node;
57 |            current != null;
58 |            current = current.getParent()) {
59 |         int type = current.getType();
60 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
61 | 
62 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
63 |           info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
64 |           visitedFunction = true;
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         } else if (current.isVar() || current.isAssign()) {
68 |           // There's one edge case we're worried about:
69 |           // if the warning points to an assigment to a function, we
70 |           // want the suppressions on that function to apply.
71 |           // It's OK if we double-count some cases.
72 |           Node rhs = NodeUtil.getRValueOfLValue(current.getFirstChild());
73 |           if (rhs != null) {
74 |             if (rhs.isCast()) {
75 |               rhs = rhs.getFirstChild();
76 |             }
77 | 
78 |             if (rhs.isFunction() && !visitedFunction) {
79 |               info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 | 
84 |         if (info != null) {
85 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
86 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
87 | 
88 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
89 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
90 |             if (guard != null) {
91 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
92 |               if (newLevel != null) {
93 |                 return newLevel;
94 |               }
95 |             }
96 |           }
97 |         }
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |     return null;
101 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CommandLineRunner.Flags.getJsFiles()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 463-469
Documentation:
/**
     * Users may specify JS inputs via the legacy {@code --js} option, as well
     * as via additional arguments to the Closure Compiler. For example, it is
     * convenient to leverage the additional arguments feature when using the
     * Closure Compiler in combination with {@code find} and {@code xargs}:
     * <pre>
     * find MY_JS_SRC_DIR -name '*.js' \
     *     | xargs java -jar compiler.jar --manage_closure_dependencies
     * </pre>
     * The {@code find} command will produce a list of '*.js' source files in
     * the {@code MY_JS_SRC_DIR} directory while {@code xargs} will convert them
     * to a single, space-delimited set of arguments that are appended to the
     * {@code java} command to run the Compiler.
     * <p>
     * Note that it is important to use the
     * {@code --manage_closure_dependencies} option in this case because the
     * order produced by {@code find} is unlikely to be sorted correctly with
     * respect to {@code goog.provide()} and {@code goog.requires()}.
     */
Method code:
463 |     List<String> getJsFiles() {
464 |       List<String> allJsInputs = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(
465 |           js.size() + arguments.size());
466 |       allJsInputs.addAll(js);
467 |       allJsInputs.addAll(arguments);
468 |       return allJsInputs;
469 |     }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.deps.SortedDependencies.topologicalStableSort(List<T>,Multimap<T, T>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/deps/SortedDependencies.java
Original range: 239-288
Documentation:

Method code:
239 |   private static <T> List<T> topologicalStableSort(
240 |       List<T> items, Multimap<T, T> deps) {
241 |     if (items.size() == 0) {
242 |       // Priority queue blows up if we give it a size of 0. Since we need
243 |       // to special case this either way, just bail out.
244 |       return Lists.newArrayList();
245 |     }
246 | 
247 |     final Map<T, Integer> originalIndex = Maps.newHashMap();
248 |     for (int i = 0; i < items.size(); i++) {
249 |       originalIndex.put(items.get(i), i);
250 |     }
251 | 
252 |     PriorityQueue<T> inDegreeZero = new PriorityQueue<T>(items.size(),
253 |         new Comparator<T>() {
254 |       @Override
255 |       public int compare(T a, T b) {
256 |         return originalIndex.get(a).intValue() -
257 |             originalIndex.get(b).intValue();
258 |       }
259 |     });
260 |     List<T> result = Lists.newArrayList();
261 | 
262 |     Multiset<T> inDegree = HashMultiset.create();
263 |     Multimap<T, T> reverseDeps = ArrayListMultimap.create();
264 |     Multimaps.invertFrom(deps, reverseDeps);
265 | 
266 |     // First, add all the inputs with in-degree 0.
267 |     for (T item : items) {
268 |       Collection<T> itemDeps = deps.get(item);
269 |       inDegree.add(item, itemDeps.size());
270 |       if (itemDeps.isEmpty()) {
271 |         inDegreeZero.add(item);
272 |       }
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     // Then, iterate to a fixed point over the reverse dependency graph.
276 |     while (!inDegreeZero.isEmpty()) {
277 |       T item = inDegreeZero.remove();
278 |       result.add(item);
279 |       for (T inWaiting : reverseDeps.get(item)) {
280 |         inDegree.remove(inWaiting, 1);
281 |         if (inDegree.count(inWaiting) == 0) {
282 |           inDegreeZero.add(inWaiting);
283 |         }
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 | 
287 |     return result;
288 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 910-932
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |       case Token.IN:
922 |         return true;
923 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
924 |         return false;
925 |     }
926 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
927 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
928 |         return true;
929 |       }
930 |     }
931 |     return false;
932 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilationLevel.applyFullCompilationOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilationLevel.java
Original range: 125-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the options that will work only if the user exported all the symbols
   * correctly.
   * @param options The CompilerOptions object to set the options on.
   */
Method code:
125 |   private static void applyFullCompilationOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
126 |     // Do not call applySafeCompilationOptions(options) because the call can
127 |     // create possible conflicts between multiple diagnostic groups.
128 | 
129 |     // All the safe optimizations.
130 |     options.dependencyOptions.setDependencySorting(true);
131 |     options.closurePass = true;
132 |     options.foldConstants = true;
133 |     options.coalesceVariableNames = true;
134 |     options.deadAssignmentElimination = true;
135 |     options.extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations = true;
136 |     options.collapseVariableDeclarations = true;
137 |     options.convertToDottedProperties = true;
138 |     options.labelRenaming = true;
139 |     options.removeDeadCode = true;
140 |     options.optimizeArgumentsArray = true;
141 |     options.collapseObjectLiterals = true;
142 |     options.protectHiddenSideEffects = true;
143 | 
144 |     // All the advanced optimizations.

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 89-115
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a module graph from a list of modules in dependency order.
   */
Method code:
89 |   public JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule> modulesInDepOrder) {
90 |     Preconditions.checkState(
91 |         modulesInDepOrder.size() == Sets.newHashSet(modulesInDepOrder).size(),
92 |         "Found duplicate modules");
93 |     modules = ImmutableList.copyOf(modulesInDepOrder);
94 |     modulesByDepth = Lists.newArrayList();
95 | 
96 |     for (JSModule module : modulesInDepOrder) {
97 |       int depth = 0;
98 |       for (JSModule dep : module.getDependencies()) {
99 |         int depDepth = dep.getDepth();
100 |         if (depDepth < 0) {
101 |           throw new ModuleDependenceException(String.format(
102 |               "Modules not in dependency order: %s preceded %s",
103 |               module.getName(), dep.getName()),
104 |               module, dep);
105 |         }
106 |         depth = Math.max(depth, depDepth + 1);
107 |       }
108 | 
109 |       module.setDepth(depth);
110 |       if (depth == modulesByDepth.size()) {
111 |         modulesByDepth.add(new ArrayList<JSModule>());
112 |       }
113 |       modulesByDepth.get(depth).add(module);
114 |     }
115 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-646
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
571 |       final JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
572 |     if (this == that) {
573 |       return true;
574 |     }
575 | 
576 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
577 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
578 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
579 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
580 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
581 |         // with everyone.
582 |         return true;
583 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
584 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
585 |         // both unknown.
586 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
587 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
588 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
589 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
590 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
591 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
592 |         return false;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
597 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
598 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
602 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
603 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
607 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
608 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
612 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
613 |       return false;
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
617 |       // TODO(johnlenz): is this valid across scopes?
618 |       return getConcreteNominalTypeName(this.toObjectType()).equals(
619 |           getConcreteNominalTypeName(that.toObjectType()));
620 |     }
621 | 
622 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
623 |       // TemplateType are they same only if they are object identical,
624 |       // which we check at the start of this function.
625 |       return false;
626 |     }
627 | 
628 |     // Unbox other proxies.
629 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
630 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
631 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
632 |               that, eqMethod);
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
636 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
637 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
638 |           eqMethod);
639 |     }
640 | 
641 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
642 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
643 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
644 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
645 |     return false;
646 |   }

===== M0381 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1919-1945
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1919 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1920 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1921 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1922 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1923 |       skipEOLs();
1924 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1925 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1926 |       skipEOLs();
1927 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1928 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1929 |       skipEOLs();
1930 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1931 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1932 |       String string = stream.getString();
1933 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1934 |         skipEOLs();
1935 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1936 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1937 |         return newStringNode(string);
1938 |       } else {
1939 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1940 |       }
1941 |     }
1942 | 
1943 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1944 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1945 |   }

===== M0387 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1453-1489
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1453 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1454 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1455 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1456 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1457 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1458 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1459 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1460 |           continue;
1461 |         }
1462 |       }
1463 | 
1464 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1465 | 
1466 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1467 |       if (n == null) {
1468 |         continue;
1469 |       }
1470 | 
1471 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1472 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1473 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1474 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1475 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1476 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1477 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1478 | 
1479 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1480 | 
1481 |         externs.add(input);
1482 |         staleInputs = true;
1483 |       }
1484 |     }
1485 | 
1486 |     if (staleInputs) {
1487 |       repartitionInputs();
1488 |     }
1489 |   }

267 |     @Override
268 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
269 |       if (n.isName()) {
270 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
271 |           case Token.VAR:
272 |           case Token.FUNCTION:
273 |           case Token.PARAM_LIST:

283 |               }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getRemainingJSDocLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 199-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
199 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
200 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
201 |     int c;
202 |     for (;;) {
203 |       c = getChar();
204 |       switch (c) {
205 |         case '*':
206 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
207 |             addToString(c);
208 |             break;
209 |           }
210 |           // fall through
211 |         case EOF_CHAR:
212 |         case '\n':
213 |           ungetChar(c);
214 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
215 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
216 |           return this.string;
217 | 
218 |         default:
219 |           addToString(c);

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.processResults(Result,List<JSModule>,B)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 804-883
Documentation:
/**
   * Processes the results of the compile job, and returns an error code.
   */
Method code:
804 |   int processResults(Result result, List<JSModule> modules, B options)
805 |        throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
806 |     if (config.printPassGraph) {
807 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
808 |         return 1;
809 |       } else {
810 |         Appendable jsOutput = createDefaultOutput();
811 |         jsOutput.append(
812 |             DotFormatter.toDot(compiler.getPassConfig().getPassGraph()));
813 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
814 |         closeAppendable(jsOutput);
815 |         return 0;
816 |       }
817 |     }
818 | 
819 |     if (config.printAst) {
820 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
821 |         return 1;
822 |       } else {
823 |         Appendable jsOutput = createDefaultOutput();
824 |         ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = compiler.computeCFG();
825 |         DotFormatter.appendDot(
826 |             compiler.getRoot().getLastChild(), cfg, jsOutput);
827 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
828 |         closeAppendable(jsOutput);
829 |         return 0;
830 |       }
831 |     }
832 | 
833 |     if (config.printTree) {
834 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
835 |         compiler.report(JSError.make(NO_TREE_GENERATED_ERROR));
836 |         return 1;
837 |       } else {
838 |         Appendable jsOutput = createDefaultOutput();
839 |         compiler.getRoot().appendStringTree(jsOutput);
840 |         jsOutput.append("\n");
841 |         closeAppendable(jsOutput);
842 |         return 0;
843 |       }
844 |     }
845 | 
846 |     rootRelativePathsMap = constructRootRelativePathsMap();
847 | 
848 |     if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
849 |       // Output the manifest and bundle files if requested.
850 |       outputManifest();
851 |       outputBundle();
852 |       outputModuleGraphJson();
853 |       return 0;
854 |     } else if (result.success) {
855 |       outputModuleGraphJson();
856 |       if (modules == null) {
857 |         outputSingleBinary();
858 | 
859 |         // Output the source map if requested.
860 |         outputSourceMap(options, config.jsOutputFile);
861 |       } else {
862 |         outputModuleBinaryAndSourceMaps(modules, options);
863 |       }
864 | 
865 |       // Output the externs if required.
866 |       if (options.externExportsPath != null) {
867 |         Writer eeOut =
868 |             openExternExportsStream(options, config.jsOutputFile);
869 |         eeOut.append(result.externExport);
870 |         eeOut.close();
871 |       }
872 | 
873 |       // Output the variable and property name maps if requested.
874 |       outputNameMaps();
875 | 
876 |       // Output the manifest and bundle files if requested.
877 |       outputManifest();
878 |       outputBundle();
879 |     }
880 | 
881 |     // return 0 if no errors, the error count otherwise
882 |     return Math.min(result.errors.length, 0x7f);
883 |   }

365 |       checks.add(printNameReferenceReport);

369 |

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 308-315
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
308 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
309 |     if (c <= 127) {
310 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
311 |     } else {
312 |       return c == 0xA0
313 |           || Character.getType((char) c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
314 |     }
315 |   }

Method code:
106 |   private void findLineOffsets() {
107 |     if (lineOffsets != null) {
108 |       return;
109 |     }
110 |     try {
111 |       String[] sourceLines = getCode().split("\n");
112 |       lineOffsets = new int[sourceLines.length];
113 |       for (int ii = 1; ii < sourceLines.length; ++ii) {
114 |         lineOffsets[ii] =
115 |             lineOffsets[ii - 1] + sourceLines[ii - 1].length() + 1;
116 |       }
117 |     } catch (IOException e) {
118 |       lineOffsets = new int[1];
119 |       lineOffsets[0] = 0;
120 |     }
121 |   }

269 |   private static boolean isAlpha(int c) {
270 |     // Use 'Z' < 'a'

1496 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
