===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0461 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.position2charno(int)

Method code:
527 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
528 |     int newlineIndex = Collections.binarySearch(newlines, position);
529 |     int lineIndex = -1;
530 |     if (newlineIndex >= 0) {
531 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(newlineIndex);
532 |     } else if (newlineIndex <= -2) {
533 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(-newlineIndex - 2);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
537 |       return position;
538 |     } else {
539 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
540 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
541 |     }
542 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 825-970
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
825 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
826 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
827 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
828 |     // that we know to be safe
829 |     switch (n.getType()) {
830 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
831 |       case Token.CAST:
832 |       case Token.AND:
833 |       case Token.BLOCK:
834 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
835 |       case Token.HOOK:
836 |       case Token.IF:
837 |       case Token.IN:
838 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
839 |       case Token.NUMBER:
840 |       case Token.OR:
841 |       case Token.THIS:
842 |       case Token.TRUE:
843 |       case Token.FALSE:
844 |       case Token.NULL:
845 |       case Token.STRING:
846 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
847 |       case Token.SWITCH:
848 |       case Token.TRY:
849 |       case Token.EMPTY:
850 |         break;
851 | 
852 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
853 |       case Token.THROW:
854 |         return true;
855 | 
856 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
857 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
858 |           return true;
859 |         }
860 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
861 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
862 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
863 |             return true;
864 |           }
865 |         }
866 |         return false;
867 | 
868 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
869 |       case Token.REGEXP:
870 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
871 |           return true;
872 |         }
873 |         break;
874 | 
875 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
876 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
877 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
878 |           return true;
879 |         }
880 |         break;
881 | 
882 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
883 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
884 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
885 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
886 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
887 | 
888 |       case Token.NEW:
889 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
890 |           return true;
891 |         }
892 | 
893 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
894 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
895 |           // side-effects
896 |           break;
897 |         }
898 |         return true;
899 | 
900 |       case Token.CALL:
901 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
902 |         // side effect property set.
903 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
904 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
905 |           // side-effects
906 |           break;
907 |         }
908 |         return true;
909 | 
910 |       default:
911 |         if (isSimpleOperator(n)) {
912 |           break;
913 |         }
914 | 
915 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
916 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
917 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
918 |             return true;
919 |           }
920 | 
921 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
922 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
923 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
924 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
925 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
926 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
927 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
928 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
929 |             return true;
930 |           }
931 | 
932 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
933 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
934 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
935 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
936 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
937 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
938 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
939 |               return false;
940 |             }
941 | 
942 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
943 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
944 |             // other objects.
945 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
946 |             // side-effect.
947 |             while (isGet(current)) {
948 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
949 |             }
950 | 
951 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
952 |           } else {
953 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
954 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
955 |             // preserve existing behavior.
956 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
957 |           }
958 |         }
959 | 
960 |         return true;
961 |     }
962 | 
963 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
964 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
965 |         return true;
966 |       }
967 |     }
968 | 
969 |     return false;
970 |   }

===== M0488 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 630-642
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 57-193
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
57 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
58 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
59 |     int c;
60 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
61 |     for (;;) {
62 |       // eat white spaces
63 |       for (;;) {
64 |         charno = -1;
65 |         c = getChar();
66 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
67 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
68 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
70 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
71 |           break;
72 |         }
73 |       }
74 | 
75 |       switch (c) {
76 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
77 |         case '@':
78 |           do {
79 |             c = getChar();
80 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
81 |               addToString(c);
82 |             } else {
83 |               ungetChar(c);
84 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
85 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
86 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
87 |             }
88 |           } while (true);
89 | 
90 |         case '*':
91 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
92 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
93 |           } else {
94 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
95 |           }
96 | 
97 |         case ',':
98 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
99 | 
100 |         case '>':
101 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
102 | 
103 |         case '(':
104 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
105 | 
106 |         case ')':
107 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
108 | 
109 |         case '{':
110 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
111 | 
112 |         case '}':
113 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
114 | 
115 |         case '[':
116 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
117 | 
118 |         case ']':
119 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
120 | 
121 |         case '?':
122 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
123 | 
124 |         case '!':
125 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
126 | 
127 |         case ':':
128 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
129 | 
130 |         case '=':
131 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
132 | 
133 |         case '|':
134 |           matchChar('|');
135 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
136 | 
137 |         case '.':
138 |           c = getChar();
139 |           if (c == '<') {
140 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
141 |           } else {
142 |             if (c == '.') {
143 |               c = getChar();
144 |               if (c == '.') {
145 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
146 |               } else {
147 |                 addToString('.');
148 |               }
149 |             }
150 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
151 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
152 |             c = '.';
153 |           }
154 |           // fall through
155 | 
156 |         default: {
157 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
158 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
159 |           int c1 = c;
160 |           addToString(c);
161 |           int c2 = getChar();
162 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
163 |             ungetChar(c2);
164 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
165 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
166 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
167 |           } else {
168 |             do {
169 |               c1 = c2;
170 |               c2 = getChar();
171 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
172 |                 ungetChar(c2);
173 |                 ungetChar(c1);
174 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
175 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
176 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
177 |               } else {
178 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
179 |                   addToString(c1);
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   ungetChar(c2);
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
184 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
185 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
186 |                 }
187 |               }
188 |             } while (true);
189 |           }
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.doRun()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 733-799
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses command-line arguments and runs the compiler.
   *
   * @return system exit status
   */
Method code:
733 |   protected int doRun() throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
734 |     Compiler.setLoggingLevel(Level.parse(config.loggingLevel));
735 | 
736 |     List<SourceFile> externs = createExterns();
737 | 
738 |     compiler = createCompiler();
739 |     B options = createOptions();
740 | 
741 |     List<JSModule> modules = null;
742 |     Result result = null;
743 | 
744 |     setRunOptions(options);
745 | 
746 |     boolean writeOutputToFile = !config.jsOutputFile.isEmpty();
747 |     List<String> outputFileNames = Lists.newArrayList();
748 |     if (writeOutputToFile) {
749 |       outputFileNames.add(config.jsOutputFile);
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     List<String> jsFiles = config.js;
753 |     List<String> moduleSpecs = config.module;
754 | 
755 |     boolean createCommonJsModules = false;
756 |     if (options.processCommonJSModules) {
757 |       if (moduleSpecs.size() == 1 && "auto".equals(moduleSpecs.get(0))) {
758 |         createCommonJsModules = true;
759 |         moduleSpecs.remove(0);
760 |       }
761 |     }
762 |     if (!moduleSpecs.isEmpty()) {
763 |       modules = createJsModules(moduleSpecs, jsFiles);
764 |       for (JSModule m : modules) {
765 |         outputFileNames.add(getModuleOutputFileName(m));
766 |       }
767 | 
768 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
769 |         compiler.initModules(externs, modules, options);
770 |       } else {
771 |         result = compiler.compileModules(externs, modules, options);
772 |       }
773 |     } else {
774 |       List<SourceFile> inputs = createSourceInputs(jsFiles);
775 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
776 |         compiler.init(externs, inputs, options);
777 |       } else {
778 |         result = compiler.compile(externs, inputs, options);
779 |       }
780 |     }
781 |     if (createCommonJsModules) {
782 |       // For CommonJS modules construct modules from actual inputs.
783 |       modules = Lists.newArrayList(compiler.getDegenerateModuleGraph()
784 |           .getAllModules());
785 |       for (JSModule m : modules) {
786 |         outputFileNames.add(getModuleOutputFileName(m));
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 | 
790 |     for (String outputFileName : outputFileNames) {
791 |       if (compiler.getSourceFileByName(outputFileName) != null) {
792 |         compiler.report(
793 |             JSError.make(OUTPUT_SAME_AS_INPUT_ERROR, outputFileName));
794 |         return 1;
795 |       }
796 |     }
797 | 
798 |     return processResults(result, modules, options);
799 |   }

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1856-1901
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1856 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1857 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1858 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1859 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1860 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 8 cases:
1861 |       // {?} - right curly
1862 |       // {?=} - equals
1863 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1864 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1865 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1866 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1867 |       // {Array.<?>} - greater than
1868 |       // /** ? */ - EOC (inline types)
1869 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1870 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1871 |       token = next();
1872 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1873 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1874 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1875 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1876 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1877 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE ||
1878 |           token == JsDocToken.GT ||
1879 |           token == JsDocToken.EOC) {
1880 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1881 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1882 |       }
1883 | 
1884 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1885 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1886 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1887 |     } else {
1888 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1889 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1890 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1891 |           next();
1892 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1893 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1894 |           next();
1895 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1896 |         }
1897 |       }
1898 | 
1899 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1900 |     }
1901 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 205-220
Documentation:
/**
   * Run all the passes in the optimizer.
   */
Method code:
205 |   @Override
206 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
207 |     progress = 0.0;
208 |     progressStep = 0.0;
209 |     if (progressRange != null) {
210 |       progressStep = (progressRange.maxValue - progressRange.initialValue)
211 |           / passes.size();
212 |       progress = progressRange.initialValue;
213 |     }
214 |     for (CompilerPass pass : passes) {
215 |       pass.process(externs, root);
216 |       if (hasHaltingErrors()) {
217 |         return;
218 |       }
219 |     }
220 |   }

   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 139-159
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   * <p>
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 1 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. The passes will run until they are finished
   * making changes.
   * <p>
   * The PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and frequency of multi-run
   * passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
139 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
140 |     Loop currentLoop = new Loop();
141 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
142 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
143 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
144 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
145 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
146 |           currentLoop = new Loop();
147 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
148 |         }
149 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
150 |       } else {
151 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
152 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
153 |       }
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
157 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0559 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 800-813
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
800 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
801 |     if (item == null) {
802 |       return null;
803 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
804 |       return item.getNext();
805 |     } else {
806 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
807 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
808 |         return item.chain(result);
809 |       } else {
810 |         return item;
811 |       }
812 |     }
813 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.ReachablePredicate.apply(EdgeTuple<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 93-111
Documentation:

Method code:
93 |     @Override
94 |     public boolean apply(EdgeTuple<Node, Branch> input) {
95 |       Branch branch = input.edge;
96 |       if (!branch.isConditional()) {
97 |         return true;
98 |       }
99 |       Node predecessor = input.sourceNode;
100 |       Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(predecessor);
101 | 
102 |       // TODO(user): Handle more complicated expression like true == true,
103 |       // etc....
104 |       if (condition != null) {
105 |         TernaryValue val = NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(condition);
106 |         if (val != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
107 |           return val.toBoolean(true) == (branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
108 |         }
109 |       }
110 |       return true;
111 |     }

===== M0571 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadFor(char)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2566-2582
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * @return Whether we found the char.
   */
Method code:
2566 |   private boolean lookAheadFor(char expect) {
2567 |     boolean matched = false;
2568 |     int c;
2569 |     while (true) {
2570 |       c = stream.getChar();
2571 |       if (c == ' ') {
2572 |         continue;
2573 |       } else if (c == expect) {
2574 |         matched = true;
2575 |         break;
2576 |       } else {
2577 |         break;
2578 |       }
2579 |     }
2580 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2581 |     return matched;
2582 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0575 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 335-389
Documentation:

Method code:
335 |   protected int getChar() {
336 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
337 |       cursor++;
338 |       --ungetCursor;
339 |       if (charno == -1) {
340 |         charno = getOffset();
341 |       }
342 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
343 |     }
344 | 
345 |     for (;;) {
346 |       int c;
347 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
348 |         hitEOF = true;
349 |         if (charno == -1) {
350 |           charno = getOffset();
351 |         }
352 |         return EOF_CHAR;
353 |       }
354 |       cursor++;
355 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
356 | 
357 | 
358 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
359 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
360 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
361 |           continue;
362 |         }
363 |         lineEndChar = -1;
364 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
365 |         lineno++;
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       if (c <= 127) {
369 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
370 |           lineEndChar = c;
371 |           c = '\n';
372 |         }
373 |       } else {
374 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
375 |           continue;
376 |         }
377 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
378 |           lineEndChar = c;
379 |           c = '\n';
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (charno == -1) {
384 |         charno = getOffset();
385 |       }
386 | 
387 |       return c;
388 |     }
389 |   }

===== M0581 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReplaceCssNames.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReplaceCssNames.java
Original range: 145-196
Documentation:

Method code:
145 |     @Override
146 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
147 |       if (n.isCall() &&
148 |           GET_CSS_NAME_FUNCTION.equals(n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName())) {
149 |         int count = n.getChildCount();
150 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
151 |         switch (count) {
152 |           case 2:
153 |             // Replace the function call with the processed argument.
154 |             if (first.isString()) {
155 |               processStringNode(t, first);
156 |               n.removeChild(first);
157 |               parent.replaceChild(n, first);
158 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
159 |             } else {
160 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
161 |                   Token.name(first.getType())));
162 |             }
163 |             break;
164 | 
165 |           case 3:
166 |             // Replace function call with concatenation of two args.  It's
167 |             // assumed the first arg has already been processed.
168 | 
169 |             Node second = first.getNext();
170 | 
171 |             if (!second.isString()) {
172 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
173 |                   Token.name(second.getType())));
174 |             } else if (first.isString()) {
175 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(
176 |                   n, UNEXPECTED_STRING_LITERAL_ERROR,
177 |                   first.getString(), second.getString()));
178 |             } else {
179 |               processStringNode(t, second);
180 |               n.removeChild(first);
181 |               Node replacement = IR.add(first,
182 |                   IR.string("-" + second.getString())
183 |                       .copyInformationFrom(second))
184 |                   .copyInformationFrom(n);
185 |               replacement.setJSType(nativeStringType);
186 |               parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
187 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
188 |             }
189 |             break;
190 | 
191 |           default:
192 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(
193 |                 n, INVALID_NUM_ARGUMENTS_ERROR, String.valueOf(count)));
194 |         }
195 |       }
196 |     }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1951-1986
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   */
Method code:
1951 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1952 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1953 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1954 |     }
1955 | 
1956 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1957 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1958 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1959 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1960 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1961 |       skipEOLs();
1962 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1963 |         next();
1964 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1965 |       }
1966 |     }
1967 | 
1968 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1969 | 
1970 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1971 |       next();
1972 |       skipEOLs();
1973 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1974 |       if (memberType != null) {
1975 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1976 | 
1977 |         skipEOLs();
1978 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1979 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1980 |         }
1981 | 
1982 |         next();
1983 |       }
1984 |     }
1985 |     return typeNameNode;
1986 |   }

===== M0608 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1248-1250
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1248 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1249 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1250 |   }

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 628-643
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
628 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
629 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
630 |       Set<String> directives = null;
631 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
632 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
633 |         if (directives == null) {
634 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
635 |         } else {
636 |           directives.add(directive);
637 |         }
638 |       }
639 | 
640 |       if (directives != null) {
641 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
642 |       }
643 |     }

851 |       }
852 |     } else if (CompilationLevel.ADVANCED_OPTIMIZATIONS == level) {
853 |       // In SIMPLE or WHITESPACE mode, if the user hasn't specified a
854 |       // translations file, they might reasonably try to write their own
855 |       // implementation of goog.getMsg that makes the substitution at
856 |       // run-time.
857 |       //
858 |       // In ADVANCED mode, goog.getMsg is going to be renamed anyway,
859 |       // so we might as well inline it. But shut off the i18n warnings,
860 |       // because the user didn't really ask for i18n.
861 |       options.messageBundle = new EmptyMessageBundle();
862 |     }
863 | 
864 |     return options;
865 |   }

===== M0658 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 567-580
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
568 |       Node ret;
569 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
570 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
571 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
572 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
573 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
574 |       } else {
575 |         ret = transform(n);
576 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
577 |       }
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
579 |       return ret;
580 |     }

===== M0661 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.createEntryPointInputs(DependencyOptions,List<CompilerInput>,SortedDependencies<CompilerInput>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 434-480
Documentation:

Method code:
434 |   private Collection<CompilerInput> createEntryPointInputs(
435 |       DependencyOptions depOptions,
436 |       List<CompilerInput> inputs,
437 |       SortedDependencies<CompilerInput> sorter)
438 |       throws MissingModuleException, MissingProvideException {
439 |     Set<CompilerInput> entryPointInputs = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
440 |     Map<String, JSModule> modulesByName = getModulesByName();
441 | 
442 |     if (depOptions.shouldPruneDependencies()) {
443 |       if (!depOptions.shouldDropMoochers()) {
444 |         entryPointInputs.addAll(sorter.getInputsWithoutProvides());
445 |       }
446 | 
447 |       for (String entryPoint : depOptions.getEntryPoints()) {
448 |         // An entry point is either formatted as:
449 |         // 'foo.bar' - peg foo.bar to its current module
450 |         // 'modC:foo.bar' - peg foo.bar to modC
451 |         String inputName = entryPoint;
452 |         int splitPoint = entryPoint.indexOf(':');
453 |         CompilerInput entryPointInput = null;
454 |         if (splitPoint != -1) {
455 |           String moduleName = entryPoint.substring(0, splitPoint);
456 |           inputName = entryPoint.substring(
457 |               Math.min(splitPoint + 1, entryPoint.length() - 1));
458 |           JSModule module = modulesByName.get(moduleName);
459 |           if (module == null) {
460 |             throw new MissingModuleException(moduleName);
461 |           } else {
462 |             entryPointInput = sorter.getInputProviding(inputName);
463 |             entryPointInput.overrideModule(module);
464 |           }
465 |         } else {
466 |           entryPointInput = sorter.getInputProviding(inputName);
467 |         }
468 | 
469 |         entryPointInputs.add(entryPointInput);
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       CompilerInput baseJs = sorter.maybeGetInputProviding("goog");
473 |       if (baseJs != null) {
474 |         entryPointInputs.add(baseJs);
475 |       }
476 |     } else {
477 |       entryPointInputs.addAll(inputs);
478 |     }
479 |     return entryPointInputs;
480 |   }

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2000-2002
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
2000 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
2001 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
2002 |   }

===== M0682 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LightweightMessageFormatter.format(JSError,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LightweightMessageFormatter.java
Original range: 68-113
Documentation:

Method code:
68 |   private String format(JSError error, boolean warning) {
69 |     // extract source excerpt
70 |     SourceExcerptProvider source = getSource();
71 |     String sourceExcerpt = source == null ? null :
72 |         excerpt.get(
73 |             source, error.sourceName, error.lineNumber, excerptFormatter);
74 | 
75 |     // formatting the message
76 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
77 |     if (error.sourceName != null) {
78 |       b.append(error.sourceName);
79 |       if (error.lineNumber > 0) {
80 |         b.append(':');
81 |         b.append(error.lineNumber);
82 |       }
83 |       b.append(": ");
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     b.append(getLevelName(warning ? CheckLevel.WARNING : CheckLevel.ERROR));
87 |     b.append(" - ");
88 | 
89 |     b.append(error.description);
90 |     b.append('\n');
91 |     if (sourceExcerpt != null) {
92 |       b.append(sourceExcerpt);
93 |       b.append('\n');
94 |       int charno = error.getCharno();
95 | 
96 |       // padding equal to the excerpt and arrow at the end
97 |       // charno == sourceExpert.length() means something is missing
98 |       // at the end of the line
99 |       if (excerpt.equals(LINE)
100 |           && 0 <= charno && charno <= sourceExcerpt.length()) {
101 |         for (int i = 0; i < charno; i++) {
102 |           char c = sourceExcerpt.charAt(i);
103 |           if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
104 |             b.append(c);
105 |           } else {
106 |             b.append(' ');
107 |           }
108 |         }
109 |         b.append("^\n");
110 |       }
111 |     }
112 |     return b.toString();
113 |   }

===== M0686 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CommandLineRunner.processArgs(String[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 657-683
Documentation:

Method code:
657 |   private List<String> processArgs(String[] args) {
658 |     // Args4j has a different format that the old command-line parser.
659 |     // So we use some voodoo to get the args into the format that args4j
660 |     // expects.
661 |     Pattern argPattern = Pattern.compile("(--[a-zA-Z_]+)=(.*)");
662 |     Pattern quotesPattern = Pattern.compile("^['\"](.*)['\"]$");
663 |     List<String> processedArgs = Lists.newArrayList();
664 | 
665 |     for (String arg : args) {
666 |       Matcher matcher = argPattern.matcher(arg);
667 |       if (matcher.matches()) {
668 |         processedArgs.add(matcher.group(1));
669 | 
670 |         String value = matcher.group(2);
671 |         Matcher quotesMatcher = quotesPattern.matcher(value);
672 |         if (quotesMatcher.matches()) {
673 |           processedArgs.add(quotesMatcher.group(1));
674 |         } else {
675 |           processedArgs.add(value);
676 |         }
677 |       } else {
678 |         processedArgs.add(arg);
679 |       }
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     return processedArgs;
683 |   }

/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.hotSwapScript(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 131-189
Documentation:

Method code:
131 |   @Override
132 |   public void hotSwapScript(Node root, Node originalRoot) {
133 |     Traversal traversal = new Traversal();
134 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, traversal);
135 | 
136 |     if (!traversal.hasErrors()) {
137 | 
138 |       // Apply the aliases.
139 |       List<AliasUsage> aliasWorkQueue =
140 |           Lists.newArrayList(traversal.getAliasUsages());
141 |       while (!aliasWorkQueue.isEmpty()) {
142 |         List<AliasUsage> newQueue = Lists.newArrayList();
143 |         for (AliasUsage aliasUsage : aliasWorkQueue) {
144 |           if (aliasUsage.referencesOtherAlias()) {
145 |             newQueue.add(aliasUsage);
146 |           } else {
147 |             aliasUsage.applyAlias();
148 |           }
149 |         }
150 | 
151 |         // Prevent an infinite loop.
152 |         if (newQueue.size() == aliasWorkQueue.size()) {
153 |           Var cycleVar = newQueue.get(0).aliasVar;
154 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(
155 |               cycleVar.getNode(), GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_CYCLE, cycleVar.getName()));
156 |           break;
157 |         } else {
158 |           aliasWorkQueue = newQueue;
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 | 
162 |       // Remove the alias definitions.
163 |       for (Node aliasDefinition : traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder()) {
164 |         if (aliasDefinition.getParent().isVar() &&
165 |             aliasDefinition.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
166 |           aliasDefinition.getParent().detachFromParent();
167 |         } else {
168 |           aliasDefinition.detachFromParent();
169 |         }
170 |       }
171 | 
172 |       // Collapse the scopes.
173 |       for (Node scopeCall : traversal.getScopeCalls()) {
174 |         Node expressionWithScopeCall = scopeCall.getParent();
175 |         Node scopeClosureBlock = scopeCall.getLastChild().getLastChild();
176 |         scopeClosureBlock.detachFromParent();
177 |         expressionWithScopeCall.getParent().replaceChild(
178 |             expressionWithScopeCall,
179 |             scopeClosureBlock);
180 |         NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(scopeClosureBlock);
181 |       }
182 | 
183 |       if (traversal.getAliasUsages().size() > 0 ||
184 |           traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder().size() > 0 ||
185 |           traversal.getScopeCalls().size() > 0) {
186 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
187 |       }
188 |     }
189 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String>,CompilerOptions,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 1281-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a map of constant names to constant values from a textual
   * description of the map.
   *
   * @param definitions A list of overriding definitions for defines in
   *     the form <name>[=<val>], where <val> is a number, boolean, or
   *     single-quoted string without single quotes.
   */
Method code:
1281 |   @VisibleForTesting
1282 |   static void createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String> definitions,
1283 |       CompilerOptions options, boolean tweaks) {
1284 |     // Parse the definitions
1285 |     for (String override : definitions) {
1286 |       String[] assignment = override.split("=", 2);
1287 |       String defName = assignment[0];
1288 | 
1289 |       if (defName.length() > 0) {
1290 |         String defValue = assignment.length == 1 ? "true" : assignment[1];
1291 | 
1292 |         boolean isTrue = defValue.equals("true");
1293 |         boolean isFalse = defValue.equals("false");
1294 |         if (isTrue || isFalse) {
1295 |           if (tweaks) {
1296 |             options.setTweakToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1297 |           } else {
1298 |             options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1299 |           }
1300 |           continue;
1301 |         } else if (defValue.length() > 1
1302 |             && ((defValue.charAt(0) == '\'' &&
1303 |                 defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\'')
1304 |                 || (defValue.charAt(0) == '\"' &&
1305 |                     defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\"'))) {
1306 |           // If the value starts and ends with a single quote,
1307 |           // we assume that it's a string.
1308 |           String maybeStringVal =
1309 |               defValue.substring(1, defValue.length() - 1);
1310 |           if (maybeStringVal.indexOf(defValue.charAt(0)) == -1) {
1311 |             if (tweaks) {
1312 |               options.setTweakToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1313 |             } else {
1314 |               options.setDefineToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1315 |             }
1316 |             continue;
1317 |           }
1318 |         } else {
1319 |           try {
1320 |             double value = Double.parseDouble(defValue);
1321 |             if (tweaks) {
1322 |               options.setTweakToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1323 |             } else {
1324 |               options.setDefineToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1325 |             }
1326 |             continue;
1327 |           } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
1328 |             // do nothing, it will be caught at the end
1329 |           }
1330 |         }
1331 |       }
1332 | 
1333 |       if (tweaks) {
1334 |         throw new RuntimeException(
1335 |             "--tweak flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1336 |       }
1337 |       throw new RuntimeException(
1338 |           "--define flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1324-1448

/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1324 |   Node parseInputs() {
1325 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1326 | 
1327 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1328 |     // individual file parse trees.
1329 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1330 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1331 |     }
1332 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1333 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1334 |     }
1335 | 
1336 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1337 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1338 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1339 | 
1340 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1341 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1342 | 
1343 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1344 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1345 | 
1346 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1347 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1348 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1349 |     }
1350 | 
1351 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1352 | 
1353 |     try {
1354 |       // Parse externs sources.
1355 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1356 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1357 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1358 |           return null;
1359 |         }
1360 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1361 |       }
1362 | 
1363 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1364 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1365 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1366 |       }
1367 | 
1368 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1369 | 
1370 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1371 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1372 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1373 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1374 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1375 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1376 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1377 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1378 |           }
1379 |         }
1380 | 
1381 |         try {
1382 |           inputs =
1383 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1384 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1385 |           staleInputs = true;
1386 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1387 |           report(JSError.make(
1388 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1389 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1390 |           report(JSError.make(
1391 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1392 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1393 |           report(JSError.make(
1394 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1395 |         }
1396 | 
1397 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1398 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1399 |           return null;
1400 |         }
1401 |       }
1402 | 
1403 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1404 | 
1405 |       if (staleInputs) {
1406 |         repartitionInputs();
1407 |       }
1408 | 
1409 |       // Build the AST.
1410 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1411 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1412 |         if (n == null) {
1413 |           continue;
1414 |         }
1415 | 
1416 |         if (devMode) {
1417 |           runSanityCheck();
1418 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1419 |             return null;
1420 |           }
1421 |         }
1422 |

1424 |         // externExportsPath.

1426 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1427 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1428 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1429 |

1431 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1432 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1433 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1434 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1435 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1436 |         }
1437 | 
1438 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1439 |       }
1440 | 
1441 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1442 |         return null;
1443 |       }
1444 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1445 |     } finally {
1446 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1447 |     }
1448 |   }

===== M0712 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 92-127
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
92 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
93 |     int cycleCount = 0;
94 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
95 | 
96 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
97 |     // doesn't converge.
98 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
99 | 
100 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
101 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
102 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
103 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
104 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
105 |     }
106 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
107 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
108 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
109 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
110 |       // on the next iteration.
111 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
112 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
113 | 
114 |       workSet.remove(source);
115 | 
116 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
117 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
118 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
119 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
120 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
121 |         }
122 |       }
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
126 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
127 |   }

===== M0733 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

/**
   * Construct and return the input root path map. The key is the exec path of
   * each input file, and the value is the corresponding root relative path.
   */
Method code:
1513 |   private Map<String, String> constructRootRelativePathsMap() {
1514 |     Map<String, String> rootRelativePathsMap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();

===== M0754 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0761 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1197-1205
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1197 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1198 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1199 |       return -1;
1200 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1202 |     } else {
1203 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1204 |     }
1205 |   }

===== M0774 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1500-1613
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1500 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1501 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1502 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1503 | 
1504 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1505 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1506 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1507 |     }
1508 | 
1509 |     stream.update();
1510 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1511 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1512 | 
1513 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1514 |     String line = getRemainingJSDocLine();
1515 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1516 |       line = line.trim();
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1520 |     builder.append(line);
1521 | 
1522 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1523 |     token = next();
1524 | 
1525 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1526 | 
1527 |     // Track the start of the line to count whitespace that
1528 |     // the tokenizer skipped. Because this case is rare, it's easier
1529 |     // to do this here than in the tokenizer.
1530 |     int lineStartChar = -1;
1531 | 
1532 |     do {
1533 |       switch (token) {
1534 |         case STAR:
1535 |           if (ignoreStar) {
1536 |             // Mark the position after the star as the new start of the line.
1537 |             lineStartChar = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1538 |           } else {
1539 |             // The star is part of the comment.
1540 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1541 |               builder.append(' ');
1542 |             }
1543 | 
1544 |             builder.append('*');
1545 |           }
1546 | 
1547 |           token = next();
1548 |           continue;
1549 | 
1550 |         case EOL:
1551 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1552 |             builder.append("\n");
1553 |           }
1554 | 
1555 |           ignoreStar = true;
1556 |           lineStartChar = 0;
1557 |           token = next();
1558 |           continue;
1559 | 
1560 |         default:
1561 |           ignoreStar = false;
1562 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1563 | 
1564 |           boolean isEOC = token == JsDocToken.EOC;
1565 |           if (!isEOC) {
1566 |             if (lineStartChar != -1 && option == WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1567 |               int numSpaces = stream.getCharno() - lineStartChar;
1568 |               for (int i = 0; i < numSpaces; i++) {
1569 |                 builder.append(' ');
1570 |               }
1571 |               lineStartChar = -1;
1572 |             } else if (builder.length() > 0) {
1573 |               // All tokens must be separated by a space.
1574 |               builder.append(' ');
1575 |             }
1576 |           }
1577 | 
1578 |           if (token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
1579 |               token == JsDocToken.EOF ||
1580 |               // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1581 |               // in the block are OK.
1582 |               (token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
1583 |                option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE)) {
1584 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1585 | 
1586 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1587 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1588 |             }
1589 | 
1590 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1591 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1592 | 
1593 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1594 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1595 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1596 |             }
1597 | 
1598 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1599 |           }
1600 | 
1601 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1602 | 
1603 |           line = getRemainingJSDocLine();
1604 | 
1605 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1606 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1607 |           }
1608 | 
1609 |           builder.append(line);
1610 |           token = next();
1611 |       }
1612 |     } while (true);
1613 |   }

===== M0790 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 82-130
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   @Override
83 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
84 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
85 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
86 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
87 |     // so we'll allow it.
88 |     if (n.isEmpty() ||
89 |         n.isComma()) {
90 |       return;
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     if (parent == null) {
94 |       return;
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     // Do not try to remove a block or an expr result. We already handle
98 |     // these cases when we visit the child, and the peephole passes will
99 |     // fix up the tree in more clever ways when these are removed.
100 |     if (n.isExprResult() || n.isBlock()) {
101 |       return;
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
105 |     // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
106 |     if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
107 |       return;
108 |     }
109 | 
110 |     boolean isResultUsed = NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(n);
111 |     boolean isSimpleOp = NodeUtil.isSimpleOperator(n);
112 |     if (!isResultUsed &&
113 |         (isSimpleOp || !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler()))) {
114 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
115 |       if (n.isString()) {
116 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
117 |       } else if (isSimpleOp) {
118 |         msg = "The result of the '" + Token.name(n.getType()).toLowerCase() +
119 |             "' operator is not being used.";
120 |       }
121 | 
122 |       t.getCompiler().report(
123 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
124 |       // TODO(johnlenz): determine if it is necessary to
125 |       // try to protect side-effect free statements as well.
126 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
127 |         problemNodes.add(n);
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 |   }
