===== M0816 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.deps.JsFileLineParser.doParse(String,Reader)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/deps/JsFileLineParser.java
Original range: 119-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Performs the line-by-line parsing of the given fileContents. This method
   * strips out JavaScript comments and then uses the abstract parseLine()
   * method to do the line parsing.
   *
   * @param filePath The path to the file being parsed. Used for reporting parse
   *     exceptions.
   * @param fileContents A reader for the contents of the file.
   */
Method code:
119 |   void doParse(String filePath, Reader fileContents) {
120 |     this.filePath = filePath;
121 |     parseSucceeded = true;
122 | 
123 |     BufferedReader lineBuffer = new BufferedReader(fileContents);
124 | 
125 |     // Parse all lines.
126 |     String line = null;
127 |     lineNum = 0;
128 |     boolean inMultilineComment = false;
129 | 
130 |     try {
131 |       while (null != (line = lineBuffer.readLine())) {
132 |         ++lineNum;
133 |         try {
134 |           String revisedLine = line;
135 |           if (inMultilineComment) {
136 |             int endOfComment = revisedLine.indexOf("*/");
137 |             if (endOfComment != -1) {
138 |               revisedLine = revisedLine.substring(endOfComment + 2);
139 |               inMultilineComment = false;
140 |             } else {
141 |               revisedLine = "";
142 |             }
143 |           }
144 | 
145 |           if (!inMultilineComment) {
146 |             while (true) {
147 |               int startOfLineComment = revisedLine.indexOf("//");
148 |               int startOfMultilineComment = revisedLine.indexOf("/*");
149 |               if (startOfLineComment != -1 &&
150 |                   (startOfMultilineComment == -1 ||
151 |                    startOfLineComment < startOfMultilineComment)) {
152 |                 revisedLine = revisedLine.substring(0, startOfLineComment);
153 |                 break;
154 |               } else if (startOfMultilineComment != -1) {
155 |                 int endOfMultilineComment = revisedLine.indexOf("*/",
156 |                     startOfMultilineComment + 2);
157 |                 if (endOfMultilineComment == -1) {
158 |                   revisedLine = revisedLine.substring(
159 |                       0, startOfMultilineComment);
160 |                   inMultilineComment = true;
161 |                   break;
162 |                 } else {
163 |                   revisedLine =
164 |                       revisedLine.substring(0, startOfMultilineComment) +
165 |                       revisedLine.substring(endOfMultilineComment + 2);
166 |                 }
167 |               } else {
168 |                 break;
169 |               }
170 |             }
171 |           }
172 | 
173 |           if (!revisedLine.isEmpty()) {
174 |             // This check for shortcut mode should be redundant, but
175 |             // it's done for safety reasons.
176 |             if (!parseLine(revisedLine) && shortcutMode) {
177 |               break;
178 |             }
179 |           }
180 |         } catch (ParseException e) {
181 |           // Inform the error handler of the exception.
182 |           errorManager.report(
183 |               e.isFatal() ? CheckLevel.ERROR : CheckLevel.WARNING,
184 |               JSError.make(filePath, lineNum, 0 /* char offset */,
185 |                   e.isFatal() ? PARSE_ERROR : PARSE_WARNING,
186 |                   e.getMessage(), line));
187 |           parseSucceeded = parseSucceeded && !e.isFatal();
188 |         }
189 |       }
190 |     } catch (IOException e) {
191 |       errorManager.report(CheckLevel.ERROR,
192 |           JSError.make(filePath, 0, 0 /* char offset */,
193 |               PARSE_ERROR, "Error reading file: " + filePath));
194 |       parseSucceeded = false;
195 |     }
196 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
2435 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2436 |     do {
2437 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
2438 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
2439 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
2440 |         return token;
2441 |       }
2442 |       token = next();
2443 |     } while (true);
2444 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
291 |   private JSDocInfo handleJsDoc(AstNode node, Node irNode) {
292 |     Comment comment = node.getJsDocNode();
293 |     if (comment != null) {
294 |       JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser = createJsDocInfoParser(comment, irNode);
295 |       parsedComments.add(comment);
296 |       if (!handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(jsDocParser)) {
297 |         JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
298 |         if (info != null) {
299 |           validateTypeAnnotations(info, node);
300 |         }
301 |         return info;
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 |     return null;
305 |   }

894 |                   .split(templateInfo.string));
895 | 
896 |           if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {
897 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.disposeparameter.missing",
898 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
899 |           } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDisposesParameter(names)) {
900 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.disposeparameter.error",
901 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
902 |           }
903 | 
904 |           token = templateInfo.token;
905 |           return token;
906 |         }
907 | 
908 |         case VERSION:
909 |           ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
910 |           String version = versionInfo.string;
911 | 
912 |           if (version.length() == 0) {
913 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
914 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
915 |           } else {
916 |             if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
917 |                parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
918 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
919 |             }
920 |           }
921 | 
922 |           token = versionInfo.token;
923 |           return token;
924 | 
925 |         case CONSTANT:
926 |         case DEFINE:
927 |         case RETURN:
928 |         case PRIVATE:
929 |         case PROTECTED:
930 |         case PUBLIC:
931 |         case THIS:
932 |         case TYPE:
933 |         case TYPEDEF:
934 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
935 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
936 | 
937 |           Node typeNode = null;
938 |           boolean hasType = lookAheadForType();
939 |           boolean isAlternateTypeAnnotation =
940 |               (annotation == Annotation.PRIVATE ||
941 |                annotation == Annotation.PROTECTED ||
942 |                annotation == Annotation.PUBLIC ||
943 |                annotation == Annotation.CONSTANT);
944 |           boolean canSkipTypeAnnotation =
945 |               (isAlternateTypeAnnotation ||
946 |                annotation == Annotation.RETURN);
947 |           type = null;
948 |           if (hasType || !canSkipTypeAnnotation) {
949 |             skipEOLs();
950 |             token = next();
951 |             typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
952 | 
953 |             if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
954 |               typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
955 |             }
956 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
957 |           }
958 | 
959 |           // The error was reported during recursive descent
960 |           // recovering parsing
961 |           boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
962 |           if (!hasError) {
963 |             // Record types for @type.
964 |             // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
965 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
966 |             // @type {type}\n@private
967 |             // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
968 |             // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
969 |             // but should be mostly OK.
970 |             if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
971 |                 || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
972 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
973 |                 parser.addTypeWarning(
974 |                     "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
975 |               }
976 |             }
977 | 
978 |             switch (annotation) {
979 |               case CONSTANT:
980 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
981 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
982 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
983 |                 }
984 |                 break;
985 | 
986 |               case DEFINE:
987 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
988 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
989 |                       lineno, charno);
990 |                 }
991 |                 return recordDescription(token);
992 | 
993 |               case PRIVATE:
994 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
995 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
996 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
997 |                       lineno, charno);
998 |                 }
999 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1000 | 
1001 |               case PROTECTED:
1002 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
1003 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
1004 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
1005 |                       lineno, charno);
1006 |                 }
1007 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1008 | 
1009 |               case PUBLIC:
1010 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
1011 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
1012 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
1013 |                       lineno, charno);
1014 |                 }
1015 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1016 | 
1017 |               case RETURN:
1018 |                 if (type == null) {
1019 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
1020 |                 }
1021 | 
1022 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
1023 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1024 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1025 |                   break;
1026 |                 }
1027 | 
1028 |                 // TODO(johnlenz): The extractMultilineTextualBlock method
1029 |                 // and friends look directly at the stream, regardless of
1030 |                 // last token read, so we don't want to read the first
1031 |                 // "STRING" out of the stream.
1032 | 
1033 |                 boolean isAnnotationNext = lookAheadForAnnotation();
1034 | 
1035 |                 // Find the return's description (if applicable).
1036 |                 if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()
1037 |                     && !isAnnotationNext) {
1038 |                   ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
1039 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
1040 | 
1041 |                   String returnDescription =
1042 |                       returnDescriptionInfo.string;
1043 | 
1044 |                   if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
1045 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
1046 |                         returnDescription);
1047 |                   }
1048 | 
1049 |                   token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
1050 |                 } else {
1051 |                   token = eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
1052 |                 }
1053 |                 return token;
1054 | 
1055 |               case THIS:
1056 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
1057 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1058 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1059 |                 }
1060 |                 break;
1061 | 
1062 |               case TYPEDEF:
1063 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
1064 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1065 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1066 |                 }
1067 |                 break;
1068 |             }
1069 |           }
1070 | 
1071 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
1072 |       }
1073 |     }
1074 | 
1075 |     return next();
1076 |   }

===== M0855 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceFile.getLine(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceFile.java
Original range: 215-248
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the source line for the indicated line number.
   *
   * @param lineNumber the line number, 1 being the first line of the file.
   * @return The line indicated. Does not include the newline at the end
   *     of the file. Returns {@code null} if it does not exist,
   *     or if there was an IO exception.
   */
Method code:
215 |   public String getLine(int lineNumber) {
216 |     findLineOffsets();
217 |     if (lineNumber > lineOffsets.length) {
218 |       return null;
219 |     }
220 | 
221 |     if (lineNumber < 1) {
222 |       lineNumber = 1;
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     int pos = lineOffsets[lineNumber - 1];
226 |     String js = "";
227 |     try {
228 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, this is optimized for few warnings.
229 |       // This is probably the right trade-off, but will be slow if there
230 |       // are lots of warnings in one file.
231 |       js = getCode();
232 |     } catch (IOException e) {
233 |       return null;
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     if (js.indexOf('\n', pos) == -1) {
237 |       // If next new line cannot be found, there are two cases
238 |       // 1. pos already reaches the end of file, then null should be returned
239 |       // 2. otherwise, return the contents between pos and the end of file.
240 |       if (pos >= js.length()) {
241 |         return null;
242 |       } else {
243 |         return js.substring(pos, js.length());
244 |       }
245 |     } else {
246 |       return js.substring(pos, js.indexOf('\n', pos));
247 |     }
248 |   }

===== M0864 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 153-237
Documentation:

Method code:
153 |   @Override
154 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
155 |     if (!n.isName()) {
156 |       return;
157 |     }
158 | 
159 |     String varName = n.getString();
160 | 
161 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
162 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
163 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
164 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent));
165 |       return;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
169 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
170 |     if ((parent.isVar() ||
171 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
172 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
173 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
174 | 
175 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
179 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
180 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
181 |     if (var == null) {
182 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
183 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
184 |         // current scope.
185 |       } else {
186 |         boolean isArguments = scope.isLocal() && ARGUMENTS.equals(varName);
187 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
188 |         if (!isArguments && !(strictExternCheck && t.getInput().isExtern())) {
189 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
190 |         }
191 | 
192 |         if (sanityCheck) {
193 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
194 |         } else {
195 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
196 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
197 |               null, compiler.getSynthesizedExternsInput());
198 |         }
199 |       }
200 |       return;
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
204 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
205 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
206 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
207 |       return;
208 |     }
209 | 
210 |     // Check module dependencies.
211 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
212 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
213 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
214 |     if (!sanityCheck &&
215 |         varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
216 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
217 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
218 |       } else {
219 |         if (scope.isGlobal()) {
220 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
221 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
222 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
223 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
224 |           } else {
225 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
226 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
227 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
228 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
229 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
230 |           }
231 |         } else {
232 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
233 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
234 |         }
235 |       }
236 |     }
237 |   }

===== M0869 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0872 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 72-107
Documentation:

Method code:
72 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
73 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
74 |       if (inputId == null) {
75 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
76 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
77 |         // from the AST.
78 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
79 |       }
80 | 
81 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
82 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
83 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
84 | 
85 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
86 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
87 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
88 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
89 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       // Args: Declare function variables
93 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
94 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
95 |            a = a.getNext()) {
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
97 |         declareVar(a);
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       // Body
101 |       scanVars(body);
102 |     } else {
103 |       // It's the global block
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
105 |       scanVars(n);
106 |     }
107 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DiagnosticGroups.registerGroup(String,DiagnosticGroup)

Method code:
42 |   static DiagnosticGroup registerGroup(String name,

===== M0881 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 204-293
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   @Override
205 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
206 |     switch (n.getType()) {
207 |       case Token.CALL:
208 |         boolean isExpr = parent.isExprResult();
209 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
210 |         if (left.isGetProp()) {
211 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
212 |           if (name.isName() &&
213 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
214 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
215 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
216 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
217 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
218 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
219 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
220 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
221 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
222 |               break;
223 |             } else if ("define".equals(methodName)) {
224 |               processDefineCall(t, n, parent);
225 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
226 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
227 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
228 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
229 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
230 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
231 |               if (arg.isString()) {
232 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
233 |                 if (dot == -1) {
234 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
235 |                 } else {
236 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
237 |                 }
238 |               }
239 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
240 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
241 |               List<String> typeDecls =
242 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
243 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
244 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
245 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
246 |                 }
247 |               }
248 | 
249 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
250 |               // get compiled out.
251 |               parent.replaceChild(n, IR.number(0));
252 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
253 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
254 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
255 |             }
256 |           }
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 | 
260 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
261 |       case Token.NAME:
262 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
263 |         // object.
264 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
265 |         break;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
268 |         handleTypedefDefinition(t, n);
269 |         break;
270 | 
271 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
272 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
273 |         // error. Hoisted functions will explode if they're provided.
274 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
275 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
276 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
277 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
278 |           if (pn != null) {
279 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
280 |           }
281 |         }
282 |         break;
283 | 
284 |       case Token.GETPROP:
285 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isName() &&
286 |             !parent.isCall() &&
287 |             !parent.isAssign() &&
288 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
289 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
290 |         }
291 |         break;
292 |     }
293 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.handleCandidateProvideDefinition(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Handles a candidate definition for a goog.provided name.
   */
Method code:
406 |   private void handleCandidateProvideDefinition(
407 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
408 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
409 |       String name = null;
410 |       if (n.isName() && parent.isVar()) {
411 |         name = n.getString();
412 |       } else if (n.isAssign() &&
413 |           parent.isExprResult()) {
414 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
415 |       }
416 | 
417 |       if (name != null) {
418 |         if (parent.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE)) {
419 |           processProvideFromPreviousPass(t, name, parent);
420 |         } else {
421 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
422 |           if (pn != null) {
423 |             pn.addDefinition(parent, t.getModule());
424 |           }
425 |         }
426 |       }
427 |     }
428 |   }

401 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.NamedPass.process(Node,Node)

Method code:
261 |     @Override
262 |     public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
263 |       logger.fine(name);
264 |       if (tracker != null) {
265 |         tracker.recordPassStart(name, factory.isOneTimePass());
266 |       }
267 |       tracer = new Tracer("JSCompiler");
268 |       // Delay the creation of the actual pass until *after* all previous passes
269 |       // have been processed.
270 |       // Some precondition checks rely on this, eg, in CoalesceVariableNames.
271 |       factory.create(compiler).process(externs, root);
272 |       try {
273 |         if (progressRange == null) {
274 |           compiler.setProgress(-1, name);
275 |         } else {
276 |           progress += progressStep;
277 |           compiler.setProgress(progress, name);
278 |         }
279 |         if (tracker != null) {
280 |           tracker.recordPassStop(name, tracer.stop());
281 |         }
282 |         maybePrintAstHashcodes(name, root);
283 |         maybeSanityCheck(externs, root);
284 |       } catch (IllegalStateException e) {
285 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Remove this once the normalization checks report
286 |         // errors instead of exceptions.
287 |         throw new RuntimeException("Sanity check failed for " + name, e);
288 |       }
289 |     }

Method code:
68 |   @Override
69 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
70 |     if (NodeUtil.isReferenceName(n)) {
71 |       String name = n.getString();
72 |       if (name.equals("RegExp") && t.getScope().getVar(name) == null) {
73 |         int parentType = parent.getType();
74 |         boolean first = (n == parent.getFirstChild());
75 |         if (!((parentType == Token.NEW && first)
76 |             || (parentType == Token.CALL && first)
77 |             || (parentType == Token.INSTANCEOF && !first)
78 |             || parentType == Token.EQ || parentType == Token.NE
79 |             || parentType == Token.SHEQ || parentType == Token.SHNE
80 |             || parentType == Token.CASE
81 |             || (parentType == Token.GETPROP && first
82 |             && !REGEXP_PROPERTY_BLACKLIST.contains(
83 |             parent.getLastChild().getString())))) {
84 |           t.report(n, REGEXP_REFERENCE);
85 |           globalRegExpPropertiesUsed = true;
86 |         }
87 |       }
88 | 
89 |     // Check the syntax of regular expression patterns.
90 |     } else if (n.isRegExp()) {
91 |       String pattern = n.getFirstChild().getString();
92 |       String flags = n.getChildCount() == 2
93 |           ? n.getLastChild().getString() : "";
94 |       try {
95 |         RegExpTree.parseRegExp(pattern, flags);
96 |       } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
97 |         t.report(n, MALFORMED_REGEXP, ex.getMessage());
98 |       } catch (IndexOutOfBoundsException ex) {
99 |         t.report(n, MALFORMED_REGEXP, ex.getMessage());
100 |       }
101 |     }
102 |   }

===== M0910 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 216-239
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
216 |   boolean parse() {
217 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
218 |     skipEOLs();
219 | 
220 |     JsDocToken token = next();
221 | 
222 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
223 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
224 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
225 |       token = blockInfo.token;
226 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
227 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
228 |       }
229 |     } else {
230 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
231 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
232 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
233 |         // what it was.
234 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
235 |       }
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
239 |   }

===== M0913 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 746-786
Documentation:

Method code:
746 |   private void compileInternal() {
747 |     setProgress(0.0, null);
748 |     parse();
749 |     // 15 percent of the work is assumed to be for parsing (based on some
750 |     // minimal analysis on big JS projects, of course this depends on options)
751 |     setProgress(0.15, "parse");
752 |     if (hasErrors()) {
753 |       return;
754 |     }
755 | 
756 |     if (!precheck()) {
757 |       return;
758 |     }
759 | 
760 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
761 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
762 |       check();
763 |       return;
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
767 |       check();
768 |       if (hasErrors()) {
769 |         return;
770 |       }
771 | 
772 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
773 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
774 |         optimize();
775 |       }
776 |     }
777 | 
778 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
779 |       recordFunctionInformation();
780 |     }
781 | 
782 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
783 |       runSanityCheck();
784 |     }
785 |     setProgress(1.0, "recordFunctionInformation");
786 |   }

===== M0916 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlStructureCheck.check(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlStructureCheck.java
Original range: 54-71
Documentation:
/**
   * Reports errors for any invalid use of control structures.
   *
   * @param node Current node to check.
   */
Method code:
54 |   private void check(Node node) {
55 |     switch (node.getType()) {
56 |       case Token.WITH:
57 |         JSDocInfo info = node.getJSDocInfo();
58 |         boolean allowWith =
59 |             info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("with");
60 |         if (!allowWith) {
61 |           report(node, USE_OF_WITH);
62 |         }
63 |         break;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     for (Node bChild = node.getFirstChild(); bChild != null;) {
67 |       Node next = bChild.getNext();
68 |       check(bChild);
69 |       bChild = next;
70 |     }
71 |   }

===== M0918 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 51-74
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
53 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = t.getControlFlowGraph().getNode(n);
54 |     if (gNode != null && gNode.getAnnotation() != GraphReachability.REACHABLE) {
55 | 
56 |       // Only report error when there are some line number informations.
57 |       // There are synthetic nodes with no line number informations, nodes
58 |       // introduce by other passes (although not likely since this pass should
59 |       // be executed early) or some rhino bug.
60 |       if (n.getLineno() != -1 &&
61 |           // Allow spurious semi-colons and spurious breaks.
62 |           !n.isEmpty() && !n.isBreak()) {
63 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, level, UNREACHABLE_CODE));
64 |         // From now on, we are going to assume the user fixed the error and not
65 |         // give more warning related to code section reachable from this node.
66 |         new GraphReachability<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
67 |             t.getControlFlowGraph()).recompute(n);
68 | 
69 |         // Saves time by not traversing children.
70 |         return false;
71 |       }
72 |     }
73 |     return true;
74 |   }

===== M0923 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 510-526
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
511 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
512 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
513 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
514 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
515 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
516 |         String inputName = input.getName();
517 | 
518 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
519 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
520 |         // will get an error down the line.
521 |         inputs.add(input);
522 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
523 |       }
524 |     }
525 |     return inputs;
526 |   }

===== M0924 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 785-868
Documentation:

Method code:
785 |     @Override
786 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
787 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
788 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
789 |       if (name == null) {
790 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
791 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
792 |           errorReporter.error(
793 |             "unnamed function statement",
794 |             sourceName,
795 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
796 | 
797 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
798 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
799 |         }
800 |         name = new Name();
801 |         name.setIdentifier("");
802 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
803 |       }
804 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
805 |       // if the function has an inline return annotation, attach it
806 |       Node newName = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(name);
807 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
808 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
809 |         // declaration.
810 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
811 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
812 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
813 |         // same line as the function keyword.
814 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
815 |             functionNode.getLp();
816 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
817 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
818 |       }
819 | 
820 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
821 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
822 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
823 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
824 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
825 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
826 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
827 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
828 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
829 |       if (fnName != null) {
830 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
831 |       } else {
832 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
833 |       }
834 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
835 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
836 | 
837 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
838 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
839 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
840 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
841 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
842 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
843 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
844 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
845 |         } else {
846 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
847 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
848 |           // (an error has already been reported).
849 |           Preconditions.checkState(
850 |               config.isIdeMode
851 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
852 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
853 |         }
854 |       }
855 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
856 | 
857 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
858 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
859 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
860 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
861 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
862 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
863 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
864 |       }
865 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
866 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
867 |      return node;
868 |     }

===== M0936 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0937 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.deps.JsFileParser.parseLine(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/deps/JsFileParser.java
Original range: 137-188
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a line of JavaScript, extracting goog.provide and goog.require
   * information.
   */
Method code:
137 |   @Override
138 |   protected boolean parseLine(String line) throws ParseException {
139 |     boolean lineHasProvidesOrRequires = false;
140 | 
141 |     // Quick sanity check that will catch most cases. This is a performance
142 |     // win for people with a lot of JS.
143 |     if (line.indexOf("provide") != -1 ||
144 |         line.indexOf("require") != -1 ||
145 |         line.indexOf("addDependency") != -1) {
146 |       // Iterate over the provides/requires.
147 |       googMatcher.reset(line);
148 |       while (googMatcher.find()) {
149 |         lineHasProvidesOrRequires = true;
150 | 
151 |         if (includeGoogBase && !fileHasProvidesOrRequires) {
152 |           fileHasProvidesOrRequires = true;
153 |           requires.add("goog");
154 |         }
155 | 
156 |         // See if it's a require or provide.
157 |         char firstChar = googMatcher.group(1).charAt(0);
158 |         boolean isProvide = firstChar == 'p';
159 |         boolean isRequire = firstChar == 'r';
160 | 
161 |         if (isProvide || isRequire) {
162 |           // Parse the param.
163 |           String arg = parseJsString(googMatcher.group(2));
164 | 
165 |           // Add the dependency.
166 |           if (isRequire) {
167 |             // goog is always implicit.
168 |             // TODO(nicksantos): I'm pretty sure we don't need this anymore.
169 |             // Remove this later.
170 |             if (!"goog".equals(arg)) {
171 |               requires.add(arg);
172 |             }
173 |           } else {
174 |             provides.add(arg);
175 |           }
176 |         }
177 |       }
178 |     } else if (includeGoogBase && line.startsWith(BASE_JS_START) &&
179 |                provides.isEmpty() && requires.isEmpty()) {
180 |       provides.add("goog");
181 | 
182 |       // base.js can't provide or require anything else.
183 |       return false;
184 |     }
185 | 
186 |     return !shortcutMode || lineHasProvidesOrRequires ||
187 |         CharMatcher.WHITESPACE.matchesAllOf(line);
188 |   }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 298-321
Documentation:

Method code:
298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     try {
304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
306 | 
307 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
308 |       sourceName = "";
309 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
310 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
311 | 
312 |       for (Node root : roots) {
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
314 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       popScope();
318 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
319 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
320 |     }
321 |   }

===== M0960 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 241-286
Documentation:

Method code:
241 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
242 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
243 |     while (true) {
244 |       switch (token) {
245 |         case ANNOTATION:
246 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
247 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
248 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
249 |           } else {
250 |             token = next();
251 |           }
252 |           break;
253 | 
254 |         case EOC:
255 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
256 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
257 |           }
258 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
259 |           return true;
260 | 
261 |         case EOF:
262 |           // discard any accumulated information
263 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
264 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
265 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
266 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
267 |           return false;
268 | 
269 |         case EOL:
270 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
271 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
272 |           }
273 |           token = next();
274 |           break;
275 | 
276 |         default:
277 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
278 |             token = next();
279 |           } else {
280 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
281 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
282 |           }
283 |           break;
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 |   }

===== M0973 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0977 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

===== M0986 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1256-1316
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1256 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1257 |     // unknown
1258 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1259 |       return true;
1260 |     }
1261 |     // all type
1262 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1263 |       return true;
1264 |     }
1265 |     // equality
1266 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1267 |       return true;
1268 |     }
1269 |     // unions
1270 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1271 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1272 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1273 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1274 |           return true;
1275 |         }
1276 |       }
1277 |       return false;
1278 |     }
1279 | 
1280 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1281 |     // are not equivalent.
1282 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1283 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1284 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1285 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1286 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1287 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1288 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1289 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1290 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1291 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1292 | 
1293 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1294 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1295 |     } else {
1296 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1297 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1298 |     }
1299 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1300 |       return false;
1301 |     }
1302 | 
1303 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1304 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1305 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1306 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1307 |               thatType);
1308 |     }
1309 | 
1310 |     // proxy types
1311 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1312 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1313 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1314 |     }
1315 |     return false;
1316 |   }

===== M0989 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 127-178
Documentation:

Method code:
127 |     @Override
128 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
129 |       // null is the smallest value
130 |       if (p2 == null) {
131 |         if (p1 == null) {
132 |           return 0;
133 |         } else {
134 |           return P1_GT_P2;
135 |         }
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // check level
139 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
140 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // sourceName comparison
144 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
145 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
146 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
147 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
148 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
149 |           return sourceCompare;
150 |         }
151 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
152 |         return P1_LT_P2;
153 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
154 |         return P1_GT_P2;
155 |       }
156 |       // lineno comparison
157 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
158 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
159 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
160 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
161 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
162 |         return P1_LT_P2;
163 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
164 |         return P1_GT_P2;
165 |       }
166 |       // charno comparison
167 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
168 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
169 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
170 |         return charno1 - charno2;
171 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
172 |         return P1_LT_P2;
173 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
174 |         return P1_GT_P2;
175 |       }
176 |       // description
177 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
178 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.

   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);

1286 |       }
1287 |     }

===== M1004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalThis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalThis.java
Original range: 79-151
Documentation:
/**
   * Since this pass reports errors only when a global {@code this} keyword
   * is encountered, there is no reason to traverse non global contexts.
   */
Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
81 | 
82 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
83 |       // Don't traverse functions that are constructors or have the @this
84 |       // or @override annotation.
85 |       JSDocInfo jsDoc = getFunctionJsDocInfo(n);
86 |       if (jsDoc != null &&
87 |           (jsDoc.isConstructor() ||
88 |            jsDoc.isInterface() ||
89 |            jsDoc.hasThisType() ||
90 |            jsDoc.isOverride())) {
91 |         return false;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Don't traverse functions unless they would normally
95 |       // be able to have a @this annotation associated with them. e.g.,
96 |       // var a = function() { }; // or
97 |       // function a() {} // or
98 |       // a.x = function() {}; // or
99 |       // var a = {x: function() {}};
100 |       int pType = parent.getType();
101 |       if (!(pType == Token.BLOCK ||
102 |             pType == Token.SCRIPT ||
103 |             pType == Token.NAME ||
104 |             pType == Token.ASSIGN ||
105 | 
106 |             // object literal keys
107 |             pType == Token.STRING_KEY)) {
108 |         return false;
109 |       }
110 | 
111 |       // Don't traverse functions that are getting lent to a prototype.
112 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
113 |       if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
114 |         JSDocInfo maybeLends = gramps.getJSDocInfo();
115 |         if (maybeLends != null &&
116 |             maybeLends.getLendsName() != null &&
117 |             maybeLends.getLendsName().endsWith(".prototype")) {
118 |           return false;
119 |         }
120 |       }
121 |     }
122 | 
123 |     if (parent != null && parent.isAssign()) {
124 |       Node lhs = parent.getFirstChild();
125 | 
126 |       if (n == lhs) {
127 |         // Always traverse the left side of the assignment. To handle
128 |         // nested assignments properly (e.g., (a = this).property = c;),
129 |         // assignLhsChild should not be overridden.
130 |         if (assignLhsChild == null) {
131 |           assignLhsChild = lhs;
132 |         }
133 |       } else {
134 |         // Only traverse the right side if it's not an assignment to a prototype
135 |         // property or subproperty.
136 |         if (NodeUtil.isGet(lhs)) {
137 |           if (lhs.isGetProp() &&
138 |               lhs.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
139 |             return false;
140 |           }
141 |           Node llhs = lhs.getFirstChild();
142 |           if (llhs.isGetProp() &&
143 |               llhs.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
144 |             return false;
145 |           }
146 |         }
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 | 
150 |     return true;
151 |   }

===== M1009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1742-1777
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1742 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1743 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1744 | 
1745 |     skipEOLs();
1746 | 
1747 |     boolean restArg = false;
1748 |     token = next();
1749 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1750 |       token = next();
1751 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1752 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1753 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1754 |       }
1755 |       restArg = true;
1756 |     }
1757 | 
1758 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1759 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1760 |       skipEOLs();
1761 |       if (restArg) {
1762 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1763 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1764 |         next();
1765 |         skipEOLs();
1766 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1767 |       }
1768 | 
1769 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1770 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1771 |       } else {
1772 |         next();
1773 |       }
1774 |     }
1775 | 
1776 |     return typeNode;
1777 |   }

===== M1016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }
